\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{2}\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+\frac{1}{2{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\Delta_{t}^{i}+\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\Delta_{t-\tau}^{i}\right]^{2}\bigg]

(Young’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L(A_{1}+1)}{\psi l_{cs}^{2}}\bigg[\zeta_{3}^{2}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})+\left(\frac{3u_{cm}^{2}}{2\zeta_{3}^{2}}+2\right)\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+\left(\frac{3u_{cm}^{2}}{2\zeta_{3}^{2}}+\frac{3}{2}\right)\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[(\Delta_{t}^{i})^{2}+(\Delta_{t-\tau}^{i})^{2}\right]\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L(A_{1}+1)}{\psi l_{cs}^{2}}\bigg[\zeta_{3}^{2}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})+\left(\frac{3u_{cm}^{2}}{2\zeta_{3}^{2}}+2\right)\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+\left(\frac{3u_{cm}^{2}}{2\zeta_{3}^{2}}+\frac{3}{2}\right)\left[\Omega_{t}+\Omega_{t-\tau}\right]\bigg].

(88)

III. Bound on
T_{33}
:

Taking expectation on both sides of
T_{33}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[T_{33}]=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}),{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})]-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}(\bm{\Theta}_{t-\tau})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\langle\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}),{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t})]-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t-\tau})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l_{cs}}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau})\right\|_{s}^{\star}\cdot\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t})]-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t-\tau})\right\|_{c}

(By (
27
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l_{cs}}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau})\right\|_{s}^{\star}\cdot m_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}^{i}\|_{c}\rho^{\tau}

(Assumption
3.1
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}\alpha}{l_{cs}}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau})\right\|_{s}^{\star}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}^{i}\|_{c}

(assumption on
\tau
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}L\alpha}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}^{i}\|_{c}

(By Proposition
B.1
and (
27
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}L\alpha}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}\left[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}^{i}\|_{c}\right]

(tringle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}L\alpha}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}\left[\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}^{i}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]\right]

(Young’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}L\alpha}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[3\left(\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\right\|_{c}^{2}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{t-\tau}\right]

(
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{m_{1}L\alpha}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[6u_{cm}^{2}M({\bm{\theta}}_{t})+3\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\right\|_{c}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{t-\tau}\right].

(89)

Substituting the bounds on
T_{31},T_{32},T_{33}
from (
84
), (
88
), (
89
), in (
80
), we get the result.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.4
.

Denote
T_{4}=\langle\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}_{t}}),{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}(\bm{\Theta}_{t})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\rangle
.
By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle T_{4}\leq\underbrace{\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\|_{s}^{\star}}_{T_{41}}\cdot\underbrace{\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}(\bm{\Theta}_{t})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\|_{s}}_{T_{42}}.

(90)

For
T_{41}
we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\|_{s}^{\star}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla{M}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})-\nabla{M}(\mathbf{0})\|_{s}^{\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L}{\psi}\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\|_{s}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L}{l_{cs}\psi}\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\|_{c}

(By (
27
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Lu_{cm}}{l_{cs}\psi}\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\|_{m}

(Proposition
B.1
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{Lu_{cm}}{l_{cs}\psi}\sqrt{2M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})}.

(91)

For
T_{42}
we have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}(\bm{\Theta}_{t})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\|_{s}=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left({\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\right)\right\|_{s}\leq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\right\|_{s}

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l_{cs}{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}})\right\|_{c}\leq\frac{\gamma_{c}}{l_{cs}{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c},

(92)

where (
92
) follows from Assumption
3.2
.
Combining (
91
) and (
92
), for an arbitrary
\zeta_{4}>0
, we get

\displaystyle T_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Lu_{cm}}{l_{cs}\psi}\sqrt{2M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})}\cdot\frac{\gamma_{c}}{l_{cs}{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\zeta_{4}\sqrt{2M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})}\cdot\frac{1}{\zeta_{4}}\left\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}\cdot\frac{Lu_{cm}}{l_{cs}^{2}\psi}

(
\gamma_{c}\leq 1
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\zeta_{4}^{2}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})+\frac{1}{2\zeta_{4}^{2}}\left\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\cdot\frac{L^{2}u_{cm}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{4}\psi^{2}}\right]

(Young’s inequality)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{4}^{2}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})+\frac{L^{2}u_{cm}^{2}}{2l_{cs}^{4}\zeta_{4}^{2}\psi^{2}}\Omega_{t}.

(93)

∎

Proof of Lemma
B.5
.

Denote
T_{5}=\left\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}+\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2}
. We have

\displaystyle T_{5}=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{G}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})+\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}+\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\underbrace{\left\|\mathbf{G}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{s}^{2}}_{T_{51}}+3\underbrace{\left\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2}}_{T_{52}}+3\left\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2}.

(94)

For
T_{51}
we have

\displaystyle T_{51}\leq

\displaystyle l_{cs}^{-2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{G}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{c}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}\right)^{2}

(By (
27
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle l_{cs}^{-2}\left(A_{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}\right)^{2}

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2(A_{2}+1)^{2}u_{cm}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{2}}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}}).

(95)

For
T_{52}
we have

\displaystyle T_{52}=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}(\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}))\right\|_{s}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2}

(
\|\cdot\|_{s}^{2}
is convex)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}l_{cs}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right\|_{c}^{2}

(By (
27
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{A_{1}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{2}}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\right\|_{c}^{2}

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{A_{1}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{2}}\Omega_{t}.

(96)

Using the bounds in (
95
), (
96
), we get

\displaystyle T_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6(A_{2}+1)^{2}u_{cm}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{2}}M({\bm{\theta}_{t}})+\frac{3A_{1}^{2}}{l_{cs}^{2}}\Omega_{t}+3\left\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right\|_{s}^{2},

(97)

where the last inequality is by the assumption on
\tau
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.6
.

Define
\theta_{l}=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\right\|_{c}
.
By the update rule of Algorithm
1
, if
l+1~\text{mod}~K\neq 0
,
we have

\displaystyle\theta_{l+1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}^{i}\right\|_{c}=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}+\alpha(\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}+\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i}))\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\theta_{l}+\frac{\alpha}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\|_{c}+\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\|_{c}\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\theta_{l}+\alpha\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[(A_{2}+1)\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\|_{c}+B\right]

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))\theta_{l}+\alpha B.

(98)

Furthermore, if
l+1~\text{mod}~K=0
, we have
{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}^{i}=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j}+\alpha(\mathbf{G}^{j}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{j})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j}+\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{j})))
, and hence

\displaystyle\theta_{l+1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}^{i}\right\|_{c}=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j}+\alpha(\mathbf{G}^{j}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{j})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{j}+\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{j})))\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\theta_{l}+\frac{\alpha}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\|_{c}+\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\|_{c}\right],

(triangle inequality)

and the same bound as in (
98
) holds. By recursive application of (
98
), we get

\displaystyle\theta_{l+1}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{l+1}\theta_{0}+\alpha B\sum_{\ell=0}^{l}(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{\ell}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{l+1}\theta_{0}+\alpha B\frac{(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{l+1}-1}{\alpha(A_{2}+1)}.

(99)

Notice that for
x\leq\frac{\log 2}{\tau}
, we have
(1+x)^{\tau+1}\leq 1+2x(\tau+1)
. If
0\leq l\leq 2\tau-1
, by the assumption on
\alpha
, we have
(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{l+1}\leq(1+\alpha(A_{2}+1))^{2\tau}\leq 1+4\alpha(A_{2}+1)\tau\leq 2
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\theta_{l}\leq

\displaystyle 2\theta_{0}+\alpha B\frac{4\alpha(A_{2}+1)\tau}{\alpha(A_{2}+1)}=2\theta_{0}+4\alpha B\tau.

(100)

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\alpha\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{y}}_{l})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}+\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{l})\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{y}}_{l})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}+\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{l})\|_{c}

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}(\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i})\right\|_{c}+\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{l})\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\right\|_{c}+\alpha B

(convexity of norm)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}(A_{2}+1)\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}^{i}\right\|_{c}+\alpha B

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha(A_{2}+1)\theta_{l}+\alpha B.

(101)

Suppose
0\leq t\leq 2\tau
. We have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}\leq

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{k+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{k}\|_{c}\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}\alpha(A_{2}+1)\theta_{k}+\alpha B\right]

(By (
101
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle B\alpha t+\alpha(A_{2}+1)\sum_{k=0}^{t-1}(2\theta_{0}+4B\alpha\tau)

(By (
100
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha\tau(B+(A_{2}+1)(2\theta_{0}+4B\alpha\tau))

(
t\leq 2\tau
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{1}}\left(B+(A_{2}+1)\left(\theta_{0}+\frac{B}{2C_{1}}\right)\right).

(102)

Furthermore, by Proposition
B.1
, we have
M({\bm{\theta}}_{t})=\frac{1}{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{m}^{2}
, and hence for any
0\leq t\leq 2\tau
we have

\displaystyle M({\bm{\theta}}_{t})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2l_{cm}^{2}}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2l_{cm}^{2}}(\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}+{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2l_{cm}^{2}}(\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c})^{2}

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l_{cm}^{2}}(\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l_{cm}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{C_{1}^{2}}\left(B+(A_{2}+1)\left(\|{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}+\frac{B}{2C_{1}}\right)\right)^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{c}^{2}\right),

which proves the first claim.

Next we prove the second claim. By the update rule in
44
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|^{2}_{c}=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}+\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}(\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2})+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{G}^{i}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\right\|_{c}^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}\right)+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}\left(\left(\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{y}}_{l}^{i})\right\|_{c}\right)^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}\right)+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

(convexity of norm)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}\left(\left(\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}A_{2}\|\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l}\|_{c}\right)^{2}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}\right)+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}(A^{2}_{2}+1)\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{l},{\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\alpha^{2}(A^{2}_{2}+1)\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2},

(103)

where (
103
) follows from Lemma
B.10
.
Taking square root on both sides, we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\leq 3\alpha\sqrt{A^{2}_{2}+1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}+2\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}+2\alpha A_{1}\Delta_{l}.

(104)

Combining the above inequality with the fact that
\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}-\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\leq\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}\leq

\displaystyle(1+3\alpha\sqrt{A^{2}_{2}+1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}+2\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}+2\alpha A_{1}\Delta_{l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\alpha C_{1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}+2\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}+2\alpha A_{1}\Delta_{l},

(105)

where we denote
C_{1}=3\sqrt{A^{2}_{2}+1}
. Assuming
t-\tau\leq l\leq t
, and taking expectation on both sides, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}]\leq(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]+2\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}]+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]+2\alpha\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{N}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\rho^{(l-t+2\tau)/2}\right]+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}]

(Lemma
B.9
)

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]+2\alpha\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha\sqrt{\rho}^{l-t+\tau}\right]+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}]

(Assumption on
\tau
)

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]+\alpha c_{t}(l)+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}],

(106)

where
c_{t}(l)=2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha\sqrt{\rho}^{l-t+\tau}\right]
.
By applying this inequality recursively, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}]\leq(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]+\alpha c_{t}(l)+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{1})\left[(1+\alpha C_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l-1}\|_{c}]+\alpha c_{t}(l-1)+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l-1}]\right]+\alpha c_{t}(l)+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{1})^{l+1-t+\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+\alpha\sum_{k=t-\tau}^{l}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{l-k}c_{t}(k)+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[\sum_{k=t-\tau}^{l}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{l-k}\Delta_{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\alpha\underbrace{\sum_{k=t-\tau}^{t}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{t-k}c_{t}(k)}_{T_{1}}+2\alpha A_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\underbrace{\left[\sum_{k=t-\tau}^{t}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{t-k}\Delta_{k}\right]}_{T_{2}}.

(107)

We study
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
in (
107
) separately. For
T_{1}
we have

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau-k}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha\sqrt{\rho}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{1}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\rho}}{1+\alpha C_{1}}\right)^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{1}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}\sqrt{\rho}^{k}\right]

(
\alpha>0
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{1}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau}\frac{1}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\right].

(108)

Notice that for
x\leq\frac{\log 2}{\tau}
, we have
(1+x)^{\tau+1}\leq 1+2x(\tau+1)
. By the assumption on
\alpha
, we have
(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau+1}\leq 1+2\alpha C_{1}(\tau+1)\leq 1+4\alpha\tau C_{1}\leq 2
and
(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau}\leq 1+2\alpha C_{1}\tau\leq 1+1/2\leq 2
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq 2\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}2(\tau+1)+\frac{2\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\right].

(109)

Furthermore, for the term
T_{2}
we have

\displaystyle T_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau-k}\Delta_{t-\tau+k}\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{1})^{\tau}\Delta_{t-\tau+k}

(due to
\alpha>0
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}(1+2\alpha C_{1}\tau)\Delta_{t-\tau+k}\leq 2\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}\Delta_{t-k}.

(110)

Subtituting (
109
), (
110
) in (
107
), for every
t-\tau\leq l\leq t
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+\alpha\frac{2u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{4B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\tau+\frac{2\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\right]+4A_{1}\alpha\sum_{k=0}^{\tau}\Delta_{t-k}.

(111)

But we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{i+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{i}\|_{c}\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[\alpha C_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{i}\|_{c}+2\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{i})\|_{c}+2\alpha A_{1}\Delta_{i}\right]

(by (
104
))

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\alpha C_{1}\left[2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+\alpha\frac{2u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{4B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\tau+\frac{2\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\right]+4A_{1}\alpha\sum_{j=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-j}]\right]

(by (
111
))

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\sqrt{\rho}^{i-t+2\tau}\right]

(Lemma
B.9
)

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha A_{1}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{i}]

(assumption on
\alpha
)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\tau C_{1}\left[2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+\alpha\frac{2u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\left[\frac{4B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\tau+\frac{2\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\alpha}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\right]+4A_{1}\alpha\sum_{j=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-j}]\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha\tau\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+2\alpha^{2}\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\frac{1}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}+2\alpha A_{1}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{i}]

(assumption on
\tau
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha\tau C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}\left[8C_{1}\alpha^{2}\tau^{2}+2\alpha\tau\right]+\frac{1}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\sqrt{Bm_{4}}\left[4C_{1}\alpha^{3}\tau+2\alpha^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(4A_{1}\alpha^{2}\tau C_{1}+2\alpha A_{1})\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{i}]

(
\Delta_{i}\geq 0
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha\tau C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}]+4\alpha\tau\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\frac{B}{\sqrt{{\rm NewA}}}+\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}\frac{4\sqrt{Bm_{4}}}{1-\sqrt{\rho}}\alpha^{2}+3A_{1}\alpha\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{i}].

(112)

Furthermore, by triangle inequality, we have
\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}\leq\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}
. By assumption on
\alpha
, we have
2\alpha\tau C_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}\leq 2\alpha\tau C_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+2\alpha\tau C_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}\leq 0.5\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+2\alpha\tau C_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}
. By taking expectation on both sides, and substituting it in (
112
), we get (
48
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.7
.

From (
105
) we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\alpha C_{1})^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{4\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{4\alpha A_{1}(1+\alpha C_{1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\Delta_{l}}_{T_{2}}+\underbrace{8\alpha^{2}A_{1}\Delta_{l}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}}_{T_{3}}.

(113)

For
T_{1}
we have

\displaystyle T_{1}=

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\cdot 2\sqrt{\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+2\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}

(
ab\leq\frac{1}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2}b^{2}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\alpha\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2},

(114)

where the last inequality is by the assumption on
\alpha
. Analogously for
T_{2}
we have

\displaystyle T_{2}=

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}\cdot 2A_{1}\sqrt{\alpha(1+\alpha C_{1})}\Delta_{l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\alpha\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}.

(115)

For
T_{3}
we have

\displaystyle T_{3}=

\displaystyle 2\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}\cdot 4\alpha A_{1}\Delta_{l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+8\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}.

(116)

Combining the bounds in (
114
), (
115
), and (
116
), and noting that
(1+\alpha C_{1})^{2}\leq 1+3\alpha C_{1}
, from (
113
) we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+3\alpha C_{1})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\alpha\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}+2\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+8\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\alpha(3C_{1}+8))\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+(6\alpha^{2}+4\alpha)\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+A_{1}^{2}(12\alpha^{2}+4\alpha)\Delta_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\alpha C_{2})\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+10\alpha\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{l}^{2}.

(117)

where
C_{2}=3C_{1}+8
. Taking expectation on both sides, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}^{2}\leq(1+\alpha C_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+10\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}}_{l})\|_{c}^{2}+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+10\alpha\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}+Bm_{4}\rho^{l-t+2\tau}\right]+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}^{2}]

(Lemma
B.9
)

\displaystyle=(1+\alpha C_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}+\alpha\bar{c}_{t}(l)+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}^{2}],

(118)

where
\bar{c}_{t}(l)=10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}+Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}\rho^{l-t+\tau}\right]
. Hence, for any
t-\tau\leq l\leq t
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l+1}\|_{c}^{2}\leq(1+\alpha C_{2})\left[(1+\alpha C_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l-1}\|_{c}^{2}+\alpha\bar{c}_{t}(l-1)+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l-1}^{2}]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha\bar{c}_{t}(l)+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{l}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{2})^{l+1-t+\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+\alpha\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{l}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{l-i}\bar{c}_{t}(i)+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{l}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{l-i}\Delta_{i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+\alpha\underbrace{\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{t-i}\bar{c}_{t}(i)}_{T_{4}}+16\alpha A_{1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\underbrace{\left[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{t-i}\Delta_{i}^{2}\right]}_{T_{5}}.

(119)

For the term
T_{4}
we have

\displaystyle T_{4}=

\displaystyle 10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau-i}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}+Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}\rho^{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{2}}+10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}\left(\frac{\rho}{1+\alpha C_{2}}\right)^{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{2}}+10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}\rho^{i}

(
\alpha C_{2}\geq 0
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\frac{(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau+1}-1}{\alpha C_{2}}+10\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau}\frac{1}{1-\rho}.

(120)

By the same argument as in Lemma
B.6
, and by the assumption on
\alpha
, we have
(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau+1}\leq 1+2\alpha C_{2}(\tau+1)\leq 1+4\alpha\tau C_{2}\leq 2
and
(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau}\leq 1+2\alpha C_{2}\tau\leq 1+1/2\leq 2
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle T_{4}\leq 40\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\tau+\frac{Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}}{1-\rho}\right].

(121)

Furthermore, for
T_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{5}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau-i}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-\tau+i}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}(1+\alpha C_{2})^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-\tau+i}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}(1+2\alpha C_{2}\tau){\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-\tau+i}^{2}]

(assumption on
\alpha
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-i}^{2}].

(122)

Combining the bounds on
T_{4}
(
121
) and
T_{5}
(
122
) in (
119
) we have for any
t-\tau\leq l\leq t
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{l}\|_{c}^{2}\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+40\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\alpha\tau+Bm_{4}\alpha^{3}\frac{1}{1-\rho}\right]+32\alpha A_{1}^{2}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-i}^{2}].

(123)

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{i+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{i}\|_{c}\right)^{2}

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{i+1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{i}\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\left[\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{i}\|_{c}^{2}+3\alpha^{2}\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{i})\|^{2}_{c}+3\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\Delta_{i}^{2}\right].

(from (
103
))

Taking expectation on both sides, and using the bounds in Lemma
B.9
and (
123
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\tau\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\left[2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+40\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left(\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\alpha\tau+\frac{Bm_{4}\alpha^{3}}{1-\rho}\right)+32\alpha A_{1}^{2}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\Delta_{t-i}^{2}]\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3\alpha^{2}\tau\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}+Bm_{4}\rho^{i-t+2\tau}\right]+3\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\tau\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[\Delta_{i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\left[2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+40\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left(\frac{B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\alpha\tau+\frac{Bm_{4}\alpha^{3}}{1-\rho}\right)\right]+3\alpha^{2}\tau\frac{u_{cD}^{2}}{l_{cD}^{2}}\left[\frac{\tau B^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}+\frac{Bm_{4}\alpha^{2}}{1-\rho}\right]

(
\rho^{\tau}\leq\alpha^{2}
)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha^{2}A_{1}^{2}\tau(3+32\alpha\tau C_{1}^{2})\sum_{i=0}^{\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\Delta_{t-i}^{2}.

(124)

Furthermore, by triangle inequality, we have
\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}\leq\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}
. Squaring both sides, we have
\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}\leq(\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}+\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c})^{2}\leq 2\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+2\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}^{2}
. By assumption on
\alpha
, we have
2\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}\leq 4\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+4\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}^{2}\leq 0.5\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t-\tau}\|_{c}^{2}+4\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}C_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}\|_{c}^{2}
. Substituting this in (
124
) we get the result.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.8
.

For
sK+1\leq t\leq(s+1)K-1
, where
s=\lfloor t/K\rfloor
,

\displaystyle{\Omega_{t}}\triangleq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left({\Delta^{i}_{t}}\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|\left({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{sK}-{\bm{\theta}}_{sK}\right)+\alpha\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left[\left(\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}+\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right)-\left(\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}+\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right)\right]\right\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\left\|\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right]\right\|_{c}^{2}+\left\|\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left[\left(\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}\right)-\left(\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right)\right]\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

(
\because{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{sK}={\bm{\theta}}_{sK}
and Young’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}\neq t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\left\langle\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}}),\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}(t-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[u_{c2}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

(Triangle inequality, and
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{c}\leq u_{c2}\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\frac{1}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{c}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}\neq t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\left\langle\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}}),\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}(t-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left[\frac{u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{c}^{2}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right].

(
l_{c2}\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\cdot\right\|_{c}
and
Var(X)\leq E[X^{2}]
)

Taking expectation

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\Omega_{t}}\right]\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}(t-sK)B^{2}+\frac{4\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}<t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\langle\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}}),\mathbb{E}_{t^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{{\rm NewA}}(t-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{c}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}},{\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{c}^{2}\right\}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

(using Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}(t-sK)B^{2}+\frac{4\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}<t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{t^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right]\right\|_{c}\right]

(using
l_{c2}\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\cdot\right\|_{c}
)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\{A_{1}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}+A_{2}^{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right\}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}(t-sK)B^{2}+\frac{4\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}{\rm NewA}}\frac{2B}{l_{c2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}=sK\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}<t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\left[(m_{2}+m_{4})\left[\rho^{t^{\prime\prime}-t^{\prime}}\right]+m_{3}\eta_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left[\left(1+\frac{2A_{1}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\right)\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\frac{2A_{2}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{2\alpha^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}(t-sK)\left[B^{2}+\frac{4m_{2}B\rho}{1-\rho}\right]+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left[\left(1+\frac{2A_{1}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\right)\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\frac{2A_{2}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right],

(125)

where
(a)
follows since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{t^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})-\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right]\right\|_{c}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}+\left\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}_{t^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}\right)\cdot\left\{\left\|\mathbb{E}_{t^{\prime}}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right]\right\|_{c}+\left\|\mathbb{E}_{t^{\prime}}\left[\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t^{\prime\prime}})\right]\right\|_{c}\right\}\right]

(Triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2B}{l_{c2}}\cdot\left[(m_{2}+m_{4})\mathbb{E}\left[\rho^{t^{\prime\prime}-t^{\prime}}\right]+m_{3}\eta_{i}\right].

(Assumption
3.1
,
3.3
)

In addition, the inequality in
(b)
follows since

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}=sK\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime\prime}=sK\\
t^{\prime}<t^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\left[(m_{2}+m_{4})\left[\rho^{t^{\prime\prime}-t^{\prime}}\right]+m_{3}\eta_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(m_{2}+m_{4})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}=sK\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime\prime}=t^{\prime}+1\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\rho^{t^{\prime\prime}-t^{\prime}}+m_{3}(t-sK)(t-1-sK)\eta_{i}

\displaystyle=(m_{2}+m_{4})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t^{\prime}=sK\end{subarray}}^{t-1}\frac{\rho-\rho^{t-t^{\prime}}}{1-\rho}+m_{3}(t-sK)(t-1-sK)\eta_{i}

\displaystyle=(m_{2}+m_{4})\frac{\rho(t-sK)-\rho\left(1-\rho^{t-sK}\right)}{1-\rho}+m_{3}(t-sK)(t-1-sK)\eta_{i}

\displaystyle\leq(m_{2}+m_{4})\frac{\rho(t-sK)}{1-\rho}+m_{3}(t-sK)(t-1-sK)\eta_{i}.

For simplicity, we define
\tilde{A}_{1}=\frac{2A_{1}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}},\tilde{A}_{2}=\frac{2A_{2}^{2}u_{c2}^{2}}{l_{c2}^{2}},\tilde{B}=\frac{u_{c2}}{l_{c2}}B
.
Hence, (
125
) simplifies to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\Omega_{t}}\leq

\displaystyle 2\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\bigg[\underbrace{\tilde{B}^{2}\left(1+\frac{4(m_{2}+m_{4})\rho}{B(1-\rho)}+\frac{4m_{3}(t-sK-1)}{B}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\eta_{i}\right)}_{\check{B}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left((1+\tilde{A}_{1})\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\tilde{A}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right)\bigg].

(126)

Recursively applying (
126
), going back
2
steps, we see

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\Omega_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha^{2}\check{B}(t-sK)+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\tilde{A}_{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-2}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\underbrace{2\alpha^{2}(t-sK-1)\left[\check{B}+2\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-2}\left((1+\tilde{A}_{1})\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\tilde{A}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right)\right]}_{\geq\Omega_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=2\alpha^{2}\check{B}(t-sK)\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-1-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\tilde{A}_{2}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-1-sK)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-1-sK)\right]\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-2}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-1-sK)\right]\Bigg[\check{B}+\tilde{A}_{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-2}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}\Bigg].

(127)

To derive the bound for going back, in general,
j
steps (such that
t-j\geq sK
), we use an induction argument.
Suppose for going back
k(<j)
steps, the bound is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\Omega_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\check{B}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\tilde{A}_{2}\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}.

(128)

We derive the bound for
k+1
steps. For this, we further bound the last term in (
128
).

\displaystyle 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k-1}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t-k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k-1}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times 4\alpha^{2}(t-k-sK)\left[\tilde{B}^{2}\left(1+\frac{4m_{2}\rho}{B(1-\rho)}\right)+\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k-1}\left((1+\tilde{A}_{1})\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\tilde{A}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right)\right]

(using (
126
))

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)(1+\tilde{A}_{1})\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k-1}\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\prod_{\ell=1}^{k-1}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times 4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-k-sK)\left[\tilde{B}^{2}\left(1+\frac{4m_{2}\rho}{B(1-\rho)}\right)+\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-k-1}\left((1+\tilde{A}_{1})\mathbb{E}\Omega_{t^{\prime}}+\tilde{A}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}\right)\right].

(129)

Substituting (
129
) into (
128
), we see that the induction hypothesis in (
128
) holds.
We can go back as far as the last instant of synchronization,
j\leq t-sK
.
For
j=t-sK
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\Omega_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\prod_{\ell=1}^{t-1-sK}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left[\check{B}+\tilde{A}_{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}+(1+\tilde{A})\mathbb{E}\Omega_{sK}\right].

(130)

Next, using
1+x\leq e^{x}
for
x\geq 0
, we get

\displaystyle\prod_{\ell=1}^{t-1-sK}\left[1+4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{t-1-sK}4\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-\ell-sK)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left(2\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(t-sK)^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{4},

(131)

if
\alpha
is small enough such that
2\alpha^{2}(1+\tilde{A}_{1})(K-1)^{2}\leq\ln{\frac{5}{4}}
, which holds true by the assumption on the step size.
Using (
131
) in (
130
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\Omega_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 5\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\left[\check{B}+\tilde{A}_{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2}+(1+\tilde{A})\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\Omega_{sK}}_{=0}\right]

\displaystyle=5\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\check{B}+5\alpha^{2}(t-sK)\tilde{A}_{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sK}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{t^{\prime}}\right\|_{c}^{2},

(132)

which concludes the proof.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.9
.

We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\|_{c}]\leq u_{cD}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[\|\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\|_{D}]

\displaystyle=u_{cD}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sqrt{\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})^{\top}D\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle u_{cD}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})^{\top}D\mathbf{b}({\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\right]}

(concavity of square root)

\displaystyle=\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right)^{\top}D\left(\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right)\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}^{\top}D\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}^{\top}D\left(\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
j\neq i\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right)\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}^{\top}D\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right]}+\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}^{\top}D\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
j\neq i\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right)\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\|_{D}^{2}\right]}_{T_{1}}}+\frac{u_{cD}}{N}\sqrt{\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]^{\top}D{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
j\neq i\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]}_{T_{2}}}

(Assumption
3.4
)

For the term
T_{1}
we have

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[l_{cD}^{-2}\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right\|_{c}^{2}\right]\leq\frac{1}{l_{cD}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[B^{2}]

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{B^{2}N}{l_{cD}^{2}}.

(133)

For the term
T_{2}
we have

\displaystyle T_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]^{\top}D{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[-\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]^{\top}D{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]^{\top}D{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]\|_{D}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]\right\|_{D}.\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]\right\|_{D}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle l_{cD}^{-2}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\right]\right\|_{c}.\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{B}{l_{cD}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\end{subarray}}^{N}\mathbf{b}^{j}({\mathbf{y}}^{j}_{t})\right]\right\|_{c}

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{N^{2}B}{l_{cD}^{2}}m_{4}\rho^{r}.

(Assumption
3.1
)

Substituting the above term and (
133
) for
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
, we get the result in (
70
).

The proof of (
71
) follows analogously.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.10
.

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}(\bm{\Theta}_{t},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})-\mathbf{G}(\bm{{\bm{\theta}}}_{t},{\mathbf{Y}_{t}})\|^{2}_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}({\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})-{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}))\right\|_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\left\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})-{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\right\|_{c}\right)^{2}

(convexity of norm)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}A_{1}\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\|_{c}\right)^{2}

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(A_{1}\Delta_{t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}A_{1}^{2}\|{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}-{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\|_{c}^{2}

(convexity of square)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{1}^{2}\Omega_{t}.

(By definition of
\Omega_{t}
)

Furthermore, by the convexity of
(\cdot)^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}^{2}=\left(\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\Delta_{t}^{i}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}(\Delta_{t}^{i})^{2}=\Omega_{t}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
B.11
.

Since
{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(\cdot)
is convex, and there exists a norm,
\|\cdot\|_{m}
, such that
{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(x)=\frac{1}{2}\left\|x\right\|_{m}^{2}
(see Proposition
B.1
), using the chain rule of subdifferential calculus,

\displaystyle\nabla{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(x)=\left\|x\right\|_{m}u_{x},

where
u_{x}\in\partial\left\|x\right\|_{m}
is a subgradient of
\left\|x\right\|_{m}
at
x
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(x)\right\|_{m}^{\star}=\left\|x\right\|_{m}\left\|u_{x}\right\|_{m}^{\star},

where
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{m}^{\star}
is the dual norm of
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{m}
.
Since
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{m}
is convex and, as a function of
x
, is
1
-Lipschitz w.r.t.
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{m}
, we have
\left\|u_{x}\right\|_{m}^{\star}\leq 1
(see Lemma 2.6 in
[
71
]
).

Furthermore, by the convexity of the
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{m}
norm,
\left\|0\right\|_{m}\geq\left\|x\right\|_{m}+\left\langle u_{x},-x\right\rangle
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(x),x\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\|x\right\|_{m}\left\langle u_{x},x\right\rangle\geq\left\|x\right\|_{m}^{2}=2{M}_{f}^{\psi,g}(x).

∎

Appendix C
Federated TD-learning

C.1
On-policy Function Approximation

Proposition C.1
.

On-policy TD-learning with linear function approximation Algorithm
2
satisfies the following:

1.

{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}={\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}

2.

S_{t}=(S_{t}^{1},\dots,S_{t}^{\rm NewA})
and
A_{t}=(A_{t}^{1},\dots,A_{t}^{\rm NewA})

3.

{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},\dots,S_{t+n-1}^{i},A_{t+n-1}^{i},S_{t+n}^{i})
and
\mathbf{{\mathbf{Y}_{t}}}=(S_{t},A_{t},\dots,S_{t+n-1},A_{t+n-1},S_{t+n})

4.

\mu^{\pi}:
Stationary distribution of the policy
\pi
.

Furthermore, choose some arbitrary positive constant
\beta>0
. The corresponding
{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})
and
\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})
in Algorithm
2
for On-policy TD-learning with linear function approximation is as follows

1.

{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})={\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\!\left(\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\right)

2.

\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})=\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\right)

where
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
solves the projected bellman equation
\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}=\Pi_{\pi}((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})
. Furthermore, the corresponding step size
\alpha
in Algorithm
1
is
\alpha\times\beta
.

Lemma C.1
.

Consider the federated on-policy TD-learning Algorithm
2
as a special case of FeGSAM Algorithm
1
(see Proposition
C.1
). Suppose that the trajectory
\{S_{t}^{i}\}_{t=0,1,\dots}
converges geometrically fast to its stationary distribution as follows
d_{TV}(P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{0}^{i})||\mu^{i}(\cdot))\leq\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}
for all
i=1,2,\dots,{\rm NewA}
. The corresponding
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
in Assumption
3.1
for the federated TD-learning is as follows

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi}\left(\gamma^{n}(P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}}-\Phi{\bm{\theta}}\right),

(134)

where
\beta>0
is an arbitrary constant introduced in Proposition
C.1
. Furthermore, for
t\geq n+1
, we have
m_{1}=\frac{2A_{2}\bar{m}}{\bar{\rho}^{n}}
,
m_{2}=2B\bar{m}
, and
m_{3}=m_{4}=0
, where
A_{2}
and
B
are specified in Lemma
C.3
and
\bar{\rho}=\rho
.

Lemma C.2
.

Consider federated on-policy TD-learning
2
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.1
). Consider the
|\mathcal{S}|\times|\mathcal{S}|
matrix
\mathbf{U}=\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi}\left(\gamma^{n}(P^{\pi})^{n}-\mathbf{I}\right)\Phi
with eigenvalues
\{\lambda_{1},\dots,\lambda_{|\mathcal{S}|}\}
. Define
\lambda_{\max}=\max_{i}|\lambda_{i}|
and
\delta=-\max_{i}\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]>0
, where
\mathfrak{Re}[\cdot]
evaluates the real part. By choosing
\beta
large enough in the linear function (
134
), there exists a weighted 2-norm
\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{\Lambda}=\sqrt{{\bm{\theta}}^{\top}\Lambda{\bm{\theta}}}
, such that
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
is a contraction with respect to this norm, that is,
\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})\|_{\Lambda}\leq\gamma_{c}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\Lambda}
for
\gamma_{c}=1-\frac{\delta^{2}}{8\lambda_{\max}^{2}}
.

Lemma C.3
.

Consider the federated on-policy TD-learning Algorithm
2
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.1
). There exist some constants
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
, and
B
such that the properties of Assumption
3.3
are satisfied.

Lemma C.4
.

Consider the federated on-policy TD-learning Algorithm
2
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.1
). Assumption
3.4
holds for this algorithm.

C.1.1
Proofs

Proof of Proposition
C.1
.

Items
1
-
4
are by definition. Subtracting
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
from both sides of the update of the TD-learning, we have

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}}_{t+1}^{i}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t+1}}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+\alpha\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\alpha\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Big(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}(\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}(\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\alpha\beta\Bigg(\underbrace{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\right)}_{{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}

\displaystyle-{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\right)}_{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\Bigg).

which proves items
1
and
2
. Furthermore, for the synchronization part of TD-learning, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{v}}^{i}_{t}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}{\mathbf{v}}^{j}_{t}

\displaystyle\implies\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}}^{i}_{t}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}\underbrace{({\mathbf{v}}^{j}_{t}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})}_{{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}},

which is equivalent to the synchronization step in FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Notice that here we used the fact that all agents have the same fixed point
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.1
.

It is easy to observe that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})={\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\left(\gamma^{n}\phi(S_{t+n}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}-\phi(S_{t}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right).

Taking expectation with respect to the stationary distribution, we have

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\left(\gamma^{n}\phi(S_{t+n}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}-\phi(S_{t}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\left(\gamma^{n}\phi(S_{t+n}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}-\phi(S_{t}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\big|S_{t}^{i}\right]\right]

(tower property of expectation)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\left(\gamma^{n}{\mathbb{E}}[(\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t+n}^{i})|S_{t}^{i}]-(\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\left(\gamma^{n}((P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})-(\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi}\left(\gamma^{n}(P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}}-\Phi{\bm{\theta}}\right).

where
P^{\pi}
is the transition probability matrix corresponding to the policy
\pi
, and
{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi}
is a diagonal matrix with diagonal entries corresponding to elements of
\mu^{\pi}
.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left(\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right){\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},y_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|.\left\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},y_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

(
\|ax\|_{c}=|a|\|x\|_{c}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|.A_{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{c}.

(Assumption
3.3
)

For brevity, we denote
P(S_{t}^{i}=s_{t}^{i})=P(s_{t}^{i})
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}

\displaystyle\left|\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi}(s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n}^{i})-P(s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n}^{i}}\bigg|\mu^{\pi}(s_{t}^{i})\pi(a_{t}^{i}|s_{t}^{i})\mathcal{P}(s_{t+1}^{i}|s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i})\dots\mathcal{P}(s_{t+n}^{i}|s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i})

\displaystyle-P(s_{t}^{i}|S_{n}^{i})\pi(a_{t}^{i}|s_{t}^{i})\mathcal{P}(s_{t+1}^{i}|s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i})\dots\mathcal{P}(s_{t+n}^{i}|s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i})\bigg|

(
t\geq n+1
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n}^{i}}\bigg|\mu^{\pi}(s_{t}^{i})-P(s_{t}^{i}|S_{n}^{i})\bigg|\pi(a_{t}^{i}|s_{t}^{i})\mathcal{P}(s_{t+1}^{i}|s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i})\dots\mathcal{P}(s_{t+n}^{i}|s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{t}^{i}}\bigg|\mu^{\pi}(s_{t}^{i})-P(s_{t}^{i}|S_{n}^{i})\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{n}^{i})||\mu^{\pi}(\cdot))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t-n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{-n})\bar{\rho}^{t}.

As explained in
[
85
]
, the projection operator
\Pi_{\pi}
is a linear operator and can be written as
\Pi_{\pi}=\Phi(\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}\Phi)^{-1}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}
, where
{\bm{\mu}}
is a diagonal matrix with diagonal entries corresponding to the stationary distribution of the policy
\pi
. Hence, the fixed-point equation is as follows
\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}=\Phi(\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}\Phi)^{-1}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})
. Since
\Phi
is a full column matrix, we can eliminate it from both sides of the equality and further multiply both sides by
\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}\Phi
. We have
\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}=\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})
, and therefore
\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})=\mathbf{0}
, which is equivalent to
{\mathbb{E}}_{S\sim\mu^{\pi}}[\phi^{\top}(S)((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})(S)-(\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})(S)]=\mathbf{0}
. By expanding
(\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{0}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi}}[\phi^{\top}(S_{0}^{i})\sum_{l=0}^{n-1}(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma(\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})(S_{l+1}^{i})-(\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})(S_{l}^{i}))]=\mathbf{0}
, which means

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}\sim\mu^{\pi}}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}})=\mathbf{0}.

(135)

Hence

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\right\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}\sim\mu^{\pi}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})]\right\|_{c}

(By (
135
))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left(\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right)\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|\left\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|B

(Assumption
3.3
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2B\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}

∎

Proof of Lemma
C.2
.

Consider the
|\mathcal{S}|\times|\mathcal{S}|
matrix
\mathbf{U}=\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi}\left(\gamma^{n}(P^{\pi})^{n}-\mathbf{I}\right)\Phi
with eigenvalues
\{\lambda_{1},\dots,\lambda_{|\mathcal{S}|}\}
. As shown in
[
85
]
, since
\Phi
is a full-rank matrix, the real part of
\lambda_{i}
is strictly negative for all
i=1,\dots,|\mathcal{S}|
. Furthermore, define
\lambda_{\max}=\max_{i}|\lambda_{i}|
and
\delta=-\max_{i}\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]>0
, where
\mathfrak{Re}[\cdot]
evaluates the real part. Consider the matrix
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}+\frac{1}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\mathbf{U}
. It is easy to show that the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
are
\{1+\frac{\lambda_{1}}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta},\dots,1+\frac{\lambda_{|\mathcal{S}|}}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\}
. For an arbitrary
i
, the norm of the i’th eigenvalue satisfies

\displaystyle\left|1+\frac{\lambda_{i}}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right|^{2}=

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\mathfrak{Im}[\lambda_{i}]}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)+\left(\frac{\mathfrak{Im}[\lambda_{i}]}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)^{2}

(
\mathfrak{Re}[\lambda_{i}]<0
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{-\delta}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)+\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{2\lambda_{\max}^{2}/\delta}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\frac{\delta^{2}}{4\lambda_{\max}^{2}}.

Hence, all the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
are in the unit circle. By
[
7
, Page 46 footnote]
, we can find a weighted 2-norm as
\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{\Lambda}=\sqrt{{\bm{\theta}}^{\top}\Lambda{\bm{\theta}}}
such that
\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
is contraction with respect to this norm with some contraction factor
\gamma_{c}
. In particular, there exists a choice of
\Lambda
such that we have
\gamma_{c}=1-\frac{\delta^{2}}{8\lambda_{\max}^{2}}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.3
.

The existence of
A_{1}
and
A_{2}
immediately follows after observing that
{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})
is a linear function of
{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}
. Furthermore, the result on
B
follows due to
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
being bounded as shown in
[
14
]
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.

For the sake of brevity, we write “
S_{t}^{i}=s^{i}_{t}
” simply as
s^{i}_{t}
, and similarly for other random variables. We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[f({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\times g({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{j})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-11.38109pt\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i}\\
s_{t}^{j},a_{t}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}\end{subarray}}\hskip-22.76219ptP(s_{t}^{i},a_{t}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i},s_{t}^{j},a_{t}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-r})f({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\times g({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-11.38109pt\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s_{t-r}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i}\\
s_{t-r}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}\end{subarray}}\hskip-22.76219ptP(s_{t-r}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i},s_{t-r}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-r})f({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\times g({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-11.38109pt\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s_{t-r}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i}\\
s_{t-r}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}\end{subarray}}\hskip-39.83385ptP(s_{t-r}^{i},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{i},a_{t+n-1}^{i},s_{t+n}^{i}|\mathcal{F}_{t-r})P(s_{t-r}^{j},\dots,s_{t+n-1}^{j},a_{t+n-1}^{j},s_{t+n}^{j}|\mathcal{F}_{t-r})f({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})g({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[f({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\times{\mathbb{E}}_{t-r}[g({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{j})].

∎

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.

By Proposition
C.1
and Lemmas
C.1
,
C.2
,
C.3
, and
C.4
, it is clear that federated TD-learning with linear function approximation Algorithm
2
satisfies all Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
on the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Furthermore, by the proof of Theorem
B.1
, we have
w_{t}=(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2})^{-t}
, and the constant
c_{TDL}
in the sampling distribution
q_{T}^{c_{TDL}}
in Algorithm
2
is
c_{TDL}=(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2})^{-1}
. Furthermore, by choosing the step size
\alpha
small enough, we can satisfy the requirements in (
35
), (
37
), (
46
),
(
49
). By choosing
K
large enough, we can satisfy
K>\tau_{\alpha}
. Hence, the result of Theorem
B.1
holds for this algorithm with some
c_{TDL}>1
. In addition, it is easy to see that
(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2})^{-\tau_{\alpha}}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, which is a constant that can be absorbed in
\mathcal{C}_{1}^{TD_{L}}
. Finally, for the sample complexity result, we simply employ Corollary
B.1.1
.

Next, we derive the constant
c_{TDL}
. Since
\|\cdot\|_{c}=\|\cdot\|_{\Lambda}
, which is smooth, we choose
g(\cdot)=\frac{1}{2}\|\cdot\|_{\Lambda}^{2}
. Taking
\psi=1
, we have
l_{cs}=u_{cs}=1
. Therefore, we have
{\varphi}_{1}=1
, and
{\varphi}_{2}=1-\gamma_{c}
, and
c_{TDL}=\left(1-\frac{\alpha(1-\gamma_{c})}{2}\right)^{-1}
, where
\gamma_{c}
is defined in Lemma
C.2
.
∎

C.2
Off-policy Tabular Setting

In this subsection, we verify that the off-policy federated TD-learning Algorithm
3
satisfies the properties of the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. In the following,
V^{\pi}
is the solution of the Bellman equation (
136
).

\displaystyle V^{\pi}(s)=\sum_{a}\pi(a|s)\left[\mathcal{R}(s,a)+\gamma\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi}(s^{\prime})\right]

(136)

Note that
V^{\pi}
is independent of the agent sampling policy. Furthermore, we take
\|\cdot\|_{c}=\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
.

Proposition C.2
.

off-policy
n
-step federated TD-learning is equivalent to the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
with the following parameters.

1.

{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}={\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}

2.

S_{t}=(S_{t}^{1},\dots,S_{t}^{\rm NewA})
and
A_{t}=(A_{t}^{1},\dots,A_{t}^{\rm NewA})

3.

{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},\dots,S_{t+n-1}^{i},A_{t+n-1}^{i},S_{t+n}^{i})
and
\mathbf{{\mathbf{Y}_{t}}}=(S_{t},A_{t},\dots,S_{t+n-1},A_{t+n-1},S_{t+n})

4.

\mu^{i}:
Stationary distribution of the sampling policy of the
i
-th agent.

5.

{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{s}={\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right)

6.

\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{s}=\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]

Lemma C.5
.

Consider the federated off-policy TD-learning Algorithm
3
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.2
). Suppose that the trajectory
\{S_{t}^{i}\}_{t=0,1,\dots}
converges geometrically fast to its stationary distribution as follows
d_{TV}(P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{0}^{i})||\mu^{i}(\cdot))\leq\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}
for all
i=1,2,\dots,{\rm NewA}
. The corresponding
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
in Assumption
3.1
for the federated TD-learning is as follows

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})_{s}=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}(s)+\left[\gamma^{n}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{n}{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}{\bm{\theta}}\right](s).

Furthermore, for
t\geq n+1
, we have
m_{1}=\frac{2A_{2}\bar{m}}{\bar{\rho}^{n}}
, where
A_{2}
is the constant specified in Assumption
3.3
,
m_{2}=m_{3}=m_{4}=0
, and
\bar{\rho}=\rho
.

Lemma C.6
.

Consider federated off-policy TD-learning
3
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.2
). The corresponding contraction factor
\gamma_{c}
in Assumption
3.2
for this algorithm is
\gamma_{c}=1-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})
, where
\mu_{\min}=\min_{s,i}\mu^{i}(s)
.

Lemma C.7
.

Consider federated off-policy TD-learning
3
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.2
).
The constants
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
, and
B
in Assumption
3.3
can be chosen as follows:

A_{1}=A_{2}=1+(1+\gamma)\begin{cases}n&\text{ if }\ \gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}=1\\
\frac{1-(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{n}}{1-\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}&\text{ o.w.}\end{cases},\quad
and
B=\frac{2\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\begin{cases}n&\text{if}\quad\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}=1\\
\frac{1-(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{n}}{1-\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}&o.w.\end{cases}
, where
\mathfrak{I}_{\max}=\max_{s^{i},a^{i},i}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(s^{i},a^{i})
.

Lemma C.8
.

Consider federated off-policy TD-learning
3
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.2
). Assumption
3.4
holds for this algorithm.

C.2.1
Proofs

Proof of Proposition
C.2
.

Items
1
-
4
are by definition. Furthermore, by the update of the TD-learning, and subtracting
{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}
from both sides, we have

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbf{V}}_{t+1}^{i}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(s)}_{{\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}^{i}(s)}=\underbrace{{\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(s)}_{{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma{\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l}^{i})\right)\right)

\displaystyle={\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)

\displaystyle+\alpha\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\Bigg\{\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\Bigg[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma\Big(\underbrace{{\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})}_{{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l+1}^{i})}+{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Big(\underbrace{{\mathbf{V}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})}_{{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l}^{i})}+{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\Big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle={\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)+\alpha\Bigg\{\underbrace{{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right)}_{\mathbf{G}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{s}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{i}(s)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\underbrace{\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]}_{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{s}}\Bigg\},

which proves items
5
and
6
. Furthermore, for the synchronization part of TD-learning, if
t\mod K=0
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{V}}^{i}_{t}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}{\mathbf{V}}^{j}_{t}

\displaystyle\implies\underbrace{{\mathbf{V}}^{i}_{t}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}\underbrace{({\mathbf{V}}^{j}_{t}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi})}_{{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}},

which is equivalent to the synchronization step in FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Notice that here we used the fact that all agents have the same fixed point
{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.5
.

By taking expectation of
{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{s}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})_{s}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}(s)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}(s)+\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]}_{T_{l}}.

Denote
{\mathbb{E}}_{k}^{i}[\cdot]={\mathbb{E}}[\cdot|\{S^{i}_{r},A^{i}_{r}\}_{r\leq k-1},S^{i}_{k}]
. For
T_{l}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{l}=
