\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{l}^{i}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l-1}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{l}^{i}\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l}){\bm{\theta}}(S_{l+1}^{i})\right]-{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right].

Here,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{l}^{i}\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l}){\bm{\theta}}(S_{l+1}^{i})\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}P(S_{l+1}^{i}=s,A_{l}^{i}=a|S_{l}^{i})\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A_{l}^{i}=a){\bm{\theta}}(s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}P(S_{l+1}^{i}=s,A_{l}^{i}=a|S_{l}^{i})\frac{\pi(a|S_{l}^{i})}{\pi^{i}(a|S_{l}^{i})}{\bm{\theta}}(s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}\pi^{i}(a|S_{l}^{i})\mathcal{P}(s|S_{l}^{i},a)\frac{\pi(a|S_{l}^{i})}{\pi^{i}(a|S_{l}^{i})}{\bm{\theta}}(s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s,a}\mathcal{P}(s|S_{l}^{i},a)\pi(a|S_{l}^{i}){\bm{\theta}}(s)\equiv[P^{\pi}{\bm{\theta}}](S_{l}^{i}),

where
[P^{\pi}]_{s_{0},s_{1}}=\sum_{a}\mathcal{P}(s_{1}|s_{0},a)\pi(a|s_{0})
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle T_{l}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l-2}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j}){\mathbb{E}}_{l-1}^{i}\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l-1},A^{i}_{l-1})\left[\gamma(P^{\pi}{\bm{\theta}})(S_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l-2}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\gamma((P^{\pi})^{2}{\bm{\theta}})(S_{l-1}^{i})-(P^{\pi}{\bm{\theta}})(S_{l-1}^{i})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dots

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\gamma((P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})-((P^{\pi})^{l-t}{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{i}(s)\gamma^{l-t}\left[\gamma((P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}{\bm{\theta}})(s)-((P^{\pi})^{l-t}{\bm{\theta}})(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma^{l-t}\left[\gamma({\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}{\bm{\theta}})(s)-({\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t}{\bm{\theta}})(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\gamma^{l-t+1}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}{\bm{\theta}}-\gamma^{l-t}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t}{\bm{\theta}}\right](s),

where we denote
{\bm{\mu}}^{i}
as diagonal matrix with diagonal entries corresponding to the stationary distribution
\mu^{i}
.
Hence, in total we have

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})_{s}=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}(s)+\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\left[\gamma^{l-t+1}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}{\bm{\theta}}-\gamma^{l-t}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{l-t}{\bm{\theta}}\right](s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}(s)+\left[\gamma^{n}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{n}{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}{\bm{\theta}}\right](s).

Furthermore, by the same argument as in the proof of Lemma
C.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\|_{c}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{i}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|.A_{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{c},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{i}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|\leq

\displaystyle(2\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{-n})\bar{\rho}^{t},

which proves that
m_{1}=2\bar{m}A_{2}\bar{\rho}^{-n}
constant. In addition, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\right\|_{c}=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]\right]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l-1}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{l}\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l})\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right]}_{T}\right]\right\|_{c}.

For the term
T
, we have

\displaystyle T=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\pi^{i}(a|S_{l}^{i}).\frac{\pi(a|S_{l}^{i})}{\pi^{i}(a|S_{l}^{i})}\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},a)+\gamma\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|S_{l}^{i},a){\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(s^{\prime})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\pi(a|S_{l}^{i})\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},a)+\gamma\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|S_{l}^{i},a){\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(s^{\prime})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0,

which shows that
m_{2}=m_{3}=m_{4}=0
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.6
.

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}+\left[\gamma^{n+1}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{n+1}{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}{\bm{\theta}}_{1}\right]-\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+\left[\gamma^{n+1}{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(P^{\pi})^{n+1}{\bm{\theta}}_{2}-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right]\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(I-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(I-\gamma^{n+1}(P^{\pi})^{n+1})\right)({\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|I-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(I-\gamma^{n+1}(P^{\pi})^{n+1})\right\|_{\infty}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}.

(definition of matrix norm)

Since the elements of the matrix
I-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(I-\gamma^{n+1}(P^{\pi})^{n+1})
is all positive, we have
\left\|I-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(I-\gamma^{n+1}(P^{\pi})^{n+1})\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|(I-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(I-\gamma^{n+1}(P^{\pi})^{n+1}))\mathbf{1}\right\|_{\infty}=\|\mathbf{1}-{\bm{\mu}}^{i}(\mathbf{1}-\gamma^{n+1}\mathbf{1})\|_{\infty}=1-\mu_{\min}^{i}(1-\gamma^{n+1})\leq 1-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.7
.

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}}_{1},{\mathbf{y}})-{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}}_{2},{\mathbf{y}})\|_{c}

\displaystyle=\max_{s}\Bigg|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\left(\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\gamma{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right)\Bigg|

(
\because c=\infty
)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s}\left[\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s)\right|+\left|\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\gamma\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l+1}^{i})\right)-\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l}^{i})\right)\right]\right|\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s}\left[\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s)\right|+\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\gamma\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l+1}^{i})\right|+\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S_{l}^{i})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S_{l}^{i})\right|\right]\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s}\left[\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s)\right|+\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\left[\gamma\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}\right]\right]

(definition of
\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{s}\left[\left|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s)\right|+\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\mathfrak{I}_{\max}^{l-t+1}\left[\gamma\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}\right]\right]

(definition of
\mathfrak{I}_{\max}
)

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left[\gamma\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}\right]\mathfrak{I}_{\max}\sum_{l=0}^{n-1}(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{l}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left[\gamma\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}\right]\mathfrak{I}_{\max}\begin{cases}n&\text{if}\quad\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}=1\\
\frac{1-(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{n}}{1-\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}&o.w.\end{cases}

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\max_{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\dots,S_{t+n}^{i}}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{\{s=S_{t}^{i}\}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\dots,S_{t+n}^{i}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right)\left|\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right|

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\dots,S_{t+n}^{i}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathfrak{I}_{\max}^{l-t+1}\right)\left|\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})-{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\dots,S_{t+n}^{i}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathfrak{I}_{\max}^{l-t+1}\right)\left[\left|\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\right|+\gamma\left|{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l+1}^{i})\right|+\left|{\mathbf{V}}^{\pi}(S_{l}^{i})\right|\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\dots,S_{t+n}^{i}}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left(\mathfrak{I}_{\max}^{l-t+1}\right)\left[1+\frac{\gamma}{1-\gamma}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{l-t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}{1-\gamma}\begin{cases}n&\text{if}\quad\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}=1\\
\frac{1-(\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max})^{n}}{1-\gamma\mathfrak{I}_{\max}}&o.w.\end{cases}

∎

Proof of Lemma
C.8
.

The proof follows similar to Lemma
C.4
.

Proof of Theorem
6.1
.

C.2
and Lemmas

,

,

, and

, it is clear that the federated off-policy TD-learning Algorithm

,

, and

of the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Furthermore, by the proof of Theorem

, and the constant

in Algorithm

is

. In equation (

) we evaluate the exact value of

.

small enough, we can satisfy the requirements in (

), (

), (

),

, and by choosing

. Hence, the result of Theorem

holds for this algorithm.

Next, we derive the constants involved in Theorem

step by step. After deriving the constants

,

\frac{1}{1-\gamma}

\mathfrak{I}_{\max}
, and
\mu_{\min}
. Since

-norm with

. It is known that

is

smooth with respect to

norm
[
2
]
, and hence

and

. Therefore, we have
{\varphi}_{1}=\frac{1+\psi u_{cs}^{2}}{1+\psi\ell_{cs}^{2}}=\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}\leq 1+\psi
. By choosing
\psi=(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1=\frac{1+2\gamma_{c}-3\gamma_{c}^{2}}{4\gamma_{c}^{2}}\geq(1-\gamma_{c})=\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})=\Omega(\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))
, which is

, we have
{\varphi}_{1}=\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}=\sqrt{e}\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}}{\sqrt{e}+(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, and

\displaystyle{\varphi}_{2}=

\displaystyle 1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}}=1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}}{1+\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1}{\sqrt{e}}}}=1-\frac{0.5(1+\gamma_{c})e^{1/4}}{\sqrt{\sqrt{e}-1+\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}}\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle=1-\frac{0.5e^{1/4}(2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1}))}{\sqrt{\sqrt{e}-1+\left(\frac{2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})}{2-2\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})}\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle 1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{1+\psi}

\displaystyle=1-\gamma_{c}\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}}=\frac{1-\gamma_{c}}{2}=0.5\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma^{n+1})=\Omega(\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))

(137)

C.3
Off-policy Function Approximation Setting

Proposition C.3
.

Off-policy TD-learning with linear function approximation Algorithm
5
satisfies the following:

1.

{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}={\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}

2.

S_{t}=(S_{t}^{1},\dots,S_{t}^{\rm NewA})
and
A_{t}=(A_{t}^{1},\dots,A_{t}^{\rm NewA})

3.

{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},\dots,S_{t+n-1}^{i},A_{t+n-1}^{i},S_{t+n}^{i})
and
\mathbf{{\mathbf{Y}_{t}}}=(S_{t},A_{t},\dots,S_{t+n-1},A_{t+n-1},S_{t+n})

4.

\mu^{\pi^{i}_{b}}:
Stationary distribution of the behaviour policy
\pi^{i}_{b}
.

Furthermore, choose some arbitrary positive constant
\beta>0
. The corresponding
{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})
and
\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})
in Algorithm
5
for Off-policy TD-learning with linear function approximation is as follows

1.

{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})={\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\!\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\right)

2.

\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})=\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\right)
.

Furthermore, the corresponding step size
\alpha
in Algorithm
1
is
\alpha\times\beta
.

Lemma C.9
.

Consider the federated off-policy TD-learning Algorithm
5
as a special case of the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
(see Proposition
C.3
). Suppose that the trajectory
\{S_{t}^{i}\}_{t=0,1,\dots}
converges geometrically fast to its stationary distribution as follows
d_{TV}(P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{0}^{i})||\mu^{i}(\cdot))\leq\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}
for all
i=1,2,\dots,{\rm NewA}
. The corresponding
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
in Assumption
3.1
for the federated TD-learning is as follows

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi_{b}^{i}}\left((\gamma P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}}-\Phi{\bm{\theta}}\right),

(138)

where
\beta>0
is an arbitrary constant introduced in Proposition
C.3
. Furthermore, for
t\geq n+1
, the bounds in (
3
) are satisfied for some constants
m_{1},m_{2},m_{3},m_{4}\geq 0
and
\bar{\rho}=\rho
.

Lemma C.10
.

Consider federated off-policy TD-learning
5
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.3
). By choosing
\beta
large enough in the linear function (
138
), and for large enough
n
, there exists a weighted 2-norm
\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{\Lambda}=\sqrt{{\bm{\theta}}^{\top}\Lambda{\bm{\theta}}}
, such that
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
is a contraction with respect to this norm, i.e.,
\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})\|_{\Lambda}\leq\gamma_{c}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\Lambda}
for
\gamma_{c}<1
.

Lemma C.11
.

Consider the federated off-policy TD-learning Algorithm
5
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.3
). There exist some constants
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
, and
B
such that the properties of Assumption
3.3
are satisfied.

Lemma C.12
.

Consider the federated off-policy TD-learning Algorithm
5
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
C.3
). Assumption
3.4
holds for this algorithm.

C.3.1
Proofs

Proof of Proposition
C.3
.

Items
1
-
4
are by definition. Subtracting
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
from both sides of the update of the TD-learning, we have

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}}_{t+1}^{i}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t+1}}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+\alpha\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\alpha\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\Big(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})\!+\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}(\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})\!

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}(\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}^{i}_{t}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}+{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\alpha\beta\Bigg(\underbrace{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}\right)}_{{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}

\displaystyle-{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\right)}_{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})}\Bigg).

which proves items
1
and
2
. Furthermore, for the synchronization part of TD-learning, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{v}}^{i}_{t}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}{\mathbf{v}}^{j}_{t}

\displaystyle\implies\underbrace{{\mathbf{v}}^{i}_{t}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}\underbrace{({\mathbf{v}}^{j}_{t}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi})}_{{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}},

which is equivalent to the synchronization step in FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Notice that here we used the fact that all agents have the same fixed point
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.9
.

By definition, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})={\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)

Taking expectation with respect to the stationary distribution, we have

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\big|S_{t}^{i}\right]\right].

Next, we look at the individual terms in the summation. We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\big|S_{t}^{i}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t},A^{i}_{t})\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t+1},A^{i}_{t+1})\dots\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\big|S_{t}^{i}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t},A^{i}_{t})\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t+1},A^{i}_{t+1})\dots\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l})\right]\left(\!\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)\big|S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},\dots,S_{l}^{i}\right]|S_{t}^{i}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t},A^{i}_{t})\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t+1},A^{i}_{t+1})\dots\right]\left(\!\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{l},A^{i}_{l})\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}\big|S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},\dots,S_{l}^{i}\right]{\bm{\theta}}\!-\!\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}\right)|S_{t}^{i}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t},A^{i}_{t})\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{t+1},A^{i}_{t+1})\dots\right]\left(\!\gamma(P^{\pi}\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{l}^{i})\!-\!(\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{l}^{i})\right)|S_{t}^{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\!\gamma((P^{\pi})^{l-t+1}\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})\!-\!((P^{\pi})^{l-t}\Phi{\bm{\theta}})(S_{t}^{i})\right).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}\left[{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})[(\gamma P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}}-\Phi{\bm{\theta}}](S_{t}^{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}+\frac{1}{\beta}\Phi^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}^{\pi_{b}^{i}}\left(\gamma^{n}(P^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\bm{\theta}}-\Phi{\bm{\theta}}\right).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{l}^{i},A_{l}^{i})+\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}-\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}\right)}_{T_{1}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\beta}\phi(S_{t}^{i})\sum_{l=t}^{t+n-1}\gamma^{l-t}\left[\Pi_{j=t}^{l}\mathfrak{I}^{(i)}(S^{i}_{j},A^{i}_{j})\right]\left(\gamma\phi(S_{l\!+\!1}^{i})^{\top}[{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}]-\phi(S_{l}^{i})^{\top}[{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}]\right)}_{T_{2}},

where
{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}
is the unique solution satisfying
\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}=\Pi_{\Phi}^{\pi_{b}^{i}}((\mathcal{T}^{\pi})^{n}\Phi{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi})
. Hence, we have
{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}T_{1}({\mathbf{y}})=\mathbf{0}
. It can be seen that
\|T_{1}({\mathbf{y}})\|_{c}\leq c_{b}^{1TD}
for some constant
c_{b}^{1TD}
. In addition, we have
\|T_{2}\|_{c}\leq c_{b}^{2TD}\|{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}\|_{c}
, where
c_{b}^{2TD}
is some constant. Furthermore, since
{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}
is the fixed point of (
11
), we have
{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{Y}_{t}}\sim\mu_{b}}\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})=\mathbf{0}
.

We have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y_{t}^{i}}\left|\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}(y_{t}^{i})-P({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=y_{t}^{i}|{\mathbf{y}}_{0}^{i})\right|.A_{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{-n})\bar{\rho}^{t}A_{2}\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{c}.

(Similar to Lemma
C.1
)

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\right\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[T_{1}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})+T_{2}]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[T_{1}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})+T_{2}]-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}[T_{1}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{b}^{2TD}\|{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}\|+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}[T_{1}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}^{i}}}[T_{1}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{b}^{2TD}\|{\mathbf{v}}^{\pi}-{\mathbf{v}}^{i,\pi}\|+2c_{b}^{1TD}\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right]\right\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}_{t}\sim\mu^{\pi_{b}}}\left[\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{i=1}^{\rm NewA}\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right]\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle m_{4}\rho^{t}

for some constant
m_{4}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.10

and
[
14
, Proposition 3.1]
.

Appendix D
Federated
Q
-learning

In this section, we verify that the federated
Q
-learning algorithm
4
satisfies the properties of the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. In the following,
Q^{*}
is the solution to the Bellman optimality equation (
139
)

\displaystyle Q^{*}(s,a)=\mathcal{R}(s,a)+\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right].

(139)

Note that
Q^{*}
is independent of the agent’s sampling policy. Furthermore,
\|\cdot\|_{c}=\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
.

Proposition D.1
.

Federated
Q
-learning algorithm
4
is equivalent to the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
with the following parameters.

1.

{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}=Q^{i}_{t}-Q^{*}

2.

S_{t}=(S_{t}^{1},\dots,S_{t}^{\rm NewA})
and
A_{t}=(A_{t}^{1},\dots,A_{t}^{\rm NewA})

3.

{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}}=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i},S_{t+1}^{i},A_{t+1}^{i})
and
\mathbf{{\mathbf{Y}_{t}}}=(S_{t},A_{t},S_{t+1},A_{t+1})

4.

\mu^{i}:
Stationary distribution of the sampling policy of the i’th agent.

5.

{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{(s,a)}={\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)

+\mathbbm{1}_{\{S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a\}}\times\big[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})\big]

6.

\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{(s,a)}=\mathbbm{1}_{\{S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a\}}\left[\mathcal{R}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})+\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})-Q^{*}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\right]

where
\mathbbm{1}_{A}
is the indicator function corresponding to set
A
, such that
\mathbbm{1}_{A}=1
is
A
is true, and
0
otherwise.

Lemma D.1
.

Consider the federated
Q
-learning Algorithm
4
as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
D.1
). Suppose that the trajectory
\{S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i}\}_{t=0,1,\dots}
converges geometrically fast to its stationary distribution as follows
d_{TV}(P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot,A_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})||\mu^{i}(\cdot,\cdot))\leq\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}
for all
i=1,2,\dots,{\rm NewA}
. The corresponding
{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})
in Assumption
3.1
for the federated
Q
-learning is as follows

\displaystyle{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})_{(s,a)}=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}}(s,a)+\mu^{i}(s,a)\times{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}(s,a)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right].

Furthermore, we have
m_{1}=2A_{2}\bar{m}
, where
A_{2}
is specified in Lemma
D.3
,
m_{2}=m_{3}=m_{4}=0
, and
\bar{\rho}=\rho
.

Lemma D.2
.

Consider federated
Q
-learning as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
D.1
). The corresponding contraction factor
\gamma_{c}
in Assumption
3.2
for this algorithm is
\gamma_{c}=\left(1-(1-\gamma)\mu_{\min}\right)
, where
\mu_{\min}=\min_{s,a,i}\mu^{i}(s,a)

Lemma D.3
.

Consider federated
Q
-learning as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
D.1
). The constants
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
, and
B
in Assumption
3.2
are as follows:

A_{1}=A_{2}=2
and
B=\frac{2}{1-\gamma}
.

Lemma D.4
.

Consider federated
Q
-learning as a special case of FeGSAM (as specified in Proposition
D.1
). Assumption
3.4
holds for this algorithm.

D.1
Proofs

Proof of Proposition
D.1
.

Items
1
-
4
are by definition. Furthermore, by the update of the
Q
-learning, and subtracting
Q^{*}
from both sides, we have

\displaystyle\underbrace{{Q}^{i}_{t+1}(s,a)-Q^{*}(s,a)}_{{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t+1}(s,a)}=\underbrace{{Q}^{i}_{t}(s,a)-Q^{*}(s,a)}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)}

\displaystyle+\alpha\mathbbm{1}_{\{(s,a)=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\}}\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})+\gamma\max_{a}{Q}^{i}_{t}(S_{t+1}^{i},a)-{Q}^{i}_{t}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)

\displaystyle+\alpha\mathbbm{1}_{\{(s,a)=(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\}}\bigg(\mathcal{R}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})+\gamma\max_{a}\left[\underbrace{{Q}^{i}_{t}(S_{t+1}^{i},a)-Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a)}_{{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t}(S_{t+1}^{i},a)}+Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(\underbrace{{Q}^{i}_{t}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})-Q^{*}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})}_{{\bm{\theta}}^{i}_{t}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})}+Q^{*}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\right)\bigg)

(addition and subtraction)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)

\displaystyle+\alpha\bigg(\underbrace{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a\}}\times\big[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}+Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})\big]}_{{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{(s,a)}}-{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}(s,a)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\underbrace{\mathbbm{1}_{\{S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a\}}\left(\mathcal{R}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})+\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S_{t+1}^{i},a^{\prime})-Q^{*}(S_{t}^{i},A_{t}^{i})\right)}_{\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{(s,a)}}\bigg),

which proves items
5
and
6
. Furthermore, for the synchronization part of
Q
-learning, we have

\displaystyle{Q}^{i}_{t}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}{Q}^{j}_{t}

\displaystyle\implies\underbrace{{Q}^{i}_{t}-Q^{*}}_{{\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\frac{1}{{\rm NewA}}\sum_{j=1}^{\rm NewA}\underbrace{({Q}^{j}_{t}-Q^{*})}_{{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}},

which is equivalent to the synchronization step in FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Notice that here we used the fact that all agents have the same fixed point
Q^{*}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
D.1
.

{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}({\bm{\theta}})_{(s,a)}
can be found simply by taking the expectation of
{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}^{i}_{t}},{\mathbf{y}^{i}_{t}})_{(s,a)}
, defined in Proposition
D.1
, with respect to the stationary distribution
\mu^{i}
. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}})-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i}\sim\mu^{i}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\left(\mu^{i}(s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a,S_{t+1}^{i}=s^{\prime},A_{t+1}^{i}=a^{\prime}|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\right){\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\left|\mu^{i}(s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a,S_{t+1}^{i}=s^{\prime},A_{t+1}^{i}=a^{\prime}|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\right|.\left\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})\right\|_{c}

(
\|ax\|_{c}=|a|\|x\|_{c}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\left|\mu^{i}(s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a,S_{t+1}^{i}=s^{\prime},A_{t+1}^{i}=a^{\prime}|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\right|.A_{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{c}

(Assumption
D.3
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s,a,s^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\bigg|\mu^{i}(s,a)\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\pi^{i}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})-P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\pi^{i}(a^{\prime}|s^{\prime})\bigg|.A_{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{c}

(definition of transition probability)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s,a}\bigg|\mu^{i}(s,a)-P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\bigg|.A_{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mu^{i}(\cdot,\cdot),P(S_{t}^{i}=\cdot,A_{t}^{i}=\cdot|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})).A_{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{c}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2A_{2}\|{\bm{\theta}}\|_{c}\bar{m}\bar{\rho}^{t}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}[\mathbf{b}^{i}({\mathbf{y}}_{t}^{i})]\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left|P(S_{t}^{i}=s,A_{t}^{i}=a|S_{0}^{i},A_{0}^{i})\left(\mathcal{R}(s,a)+\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}[\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})]-Q^{*}(s,a)\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

(Bellman optimality equation (
139
))

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.2
.

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})\|_{c}=\max_{s,a}|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})_{(s,a)}-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})_{(s,a)}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\Bigg|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)+\mu^{i}(s,a)\times{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a)+\mu^{i}(s,a)\times{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right]\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\bigg|(1-\mu^{i}(s,a))({\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a))

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\gamma\mu^{i}(s,a){\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\Bigg[\bigg|(1-\mu^{i}(s,a))({\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a))\bigg|

\displaystyle+\gamma\bigg|\mu^{i}(s,a){\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right]\bigg|\Bigg]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\Bigg[(1-\mu^{i}(s,a))\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

(definition of
\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
)

\displaystyle+\gamma\mu^{i}(s,a)\left[\bigg|{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\left[\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right]\bigg|\right]\Bigg]

(
\mu^{i}(s,a)\geq 0
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\Bigg[(1-\mu^{i}(s,a))\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\gamma\mu^{i}(s,a)\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{S^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{P}(\cdot|s,a)}\bigg|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\bigg|\right]\Bigg]

(Jensen’s inequality)

Next, we note that for any functions
f(\cdot)
and
g(\cdot)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|(\max_{x}f(x))-(\max_{x}g(x))\right|\leq\max_{x}\left|f(x)-g(x)\right|.

(140)

The reason is as follows. We have
\max_{x}f(x)=\max_{x}f(x)-g(x)+g(x)\leq(\max_{x}f(x)-g(x))+(\max_{x}g(x))
. Hence,
(\max_{x}f(x))-(\max_{x}g(x))\leq\max_{x}f(x)-g(x)\leq\max_{x}\left|f(x)-g(x)\right|
. Now suppose
\max_{x}f(x)\geq\max_{x}g(x)
. Then we can apply absolute value to the left hand side of the inequality, and we get the bound. Using a similar argument for the case
\max_{x}f(x)\leq\max_{x}g(x)
, we get the bound in (
140
). Hence, we have
\left|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right|\leq\max_{a^{\prime}}|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})|\leq\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\infty}
. As a result, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{1})-{\bar{\mathbf{G}}}^{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{2})\|_{c}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left[(1-\mu^{i}(s,a))\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\gamma\mu^{i}(s,a)\left[\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\infty}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left(1-(1-\gamma)\mu^{i}(s,a)\right)\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-(1-\gamma)\mu^{i}_{\min}\right)\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-(1-\gamma)\mu_{\min}\right)\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-(1-\gamma)\mu_{\min}\right)\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{c}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

First, for
A_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}}_{1},{\mathbf{y}})-{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}}_{2},{\mathbf{y}})\|_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\bigg|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\times\big[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}_{1}(S,A)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\times\big[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(S,A)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\big]\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\bigg[\left|(1-\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}})({\bm{\theta}}_{1}(s,a)-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}(s,a))\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\gamma\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right|\bigg]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\bigg[\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\gamma\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{1}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}_{2}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)\right|\bigg]

(definition of
\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\bigg[\left\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\right\|_{\infty}+\gamma\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\infty}\bigg]

(By (
140
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\|{\bm{\theta}}_{1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{2}\|_{c}

Second, for
A_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{G}^{i}}({\bm{\theta}},{\mathbf{y}})\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left|{\bm{\theta}}(s,a)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\times\big[\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-{\bm{\theta}}(S,A)-\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\big]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left[\left|(1-\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}){\bm{\theta}}(a,s)\right|+\gamma\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left[\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{\infty}+\gamma\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left|\max_{a^{\prime}}\left({\bm{\theta}}+Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right)-\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right]

(definition of
\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left[\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{\infty}+\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left|{\bm{\theta}}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|\right]

(By (
140
))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left\|{\bm{\theta}}\right\|_{c}.

Lastly, for
B
, we have

\displaystyle\|b^{i}({\mathbf{y}}^{i})\|_{c}=

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left[\mathcal{R}(S,A)+\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})-Q^{*}(S,A)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\mathbbm{1}_{\{S=s,A=a\}}\left[\left|\mathcal{R}(S,A)\right|+\gamma\max_{a^{\prime}}\left|Q^{*}(S^{\prime},a^{\prime})\right|+\left|Q^{*}(S,A)\right|\right]

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{s,a}\left[1+\gamma\frac{1}{1-\gamma}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.4
.

The proof follows similar to Lemma
C.4
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
6.2
.

By Proposition
D.1
and Lemmas
D.1
,
D.2
,
D.3
, and
D.4
, it is clear that the federated
Q
Algorithm
4
satisfies all the Assumptions
3.1
,
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
of the FeGSAM Algorithm
1
. Furthermore, by the proof of Theorem
B.1
, we have
w_{t}=(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2})^{-t}
, and the constant
c
in the sampling distribution
q_{T}^{c}
in Algorithm
2
is
c=(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2})^{-1}
. In equation (
141
) we evaluate the exact value of
w_{t}
.

Furthermore, by choosing the step size
\alpha
small enough, we can satisfy the requirements in (
35
), (
37
), (
46
),
(
49
). By choosing
K
large enough, we can satisfy
K>\tau
, and by choosing
T
large enough, we can satisfy
T>K+\tau
. Hence, the result of Theorem
B.1
holds for this algorithm.

Next, we derive the constants involved in Theorem
B.1
step by step. In this analysis, we only consider the terms that involve
|\mathcal{S}|
,
|\mathcal{A}|
,
\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,
\mathfrak{I}_{\max}
, and
\mu_{\min}
. Since
\|\cdot\|_{c}=\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
, we choose
g(\cdot)=\frac{1}{2}\|\cdot\|_{p}^{2}
, that is, the
p
-norm with
p=2\log(|\mathcal{S}|)
. It is known that
g(\cdot)
is
(p-1)
smooth with respect to the
\|\cdot\|_{p}
norm
[
2
]
, and hence
L=\Theta(\log(|\mathcal{S}|))
. Hence, we have
l_{cs}=|\mathcal{S}|^{-1/p}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{e}}=\Theta(1)
and
u_{cs}=1
. Therefore, we have
{\varphi}_{1}=\frac{1+\psi u_{cs}^{2}}{1+\psi\ell_{cs}^{2}}=\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}\leq 1+\psi
. By choosing
\psi=(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1=\frac{1+2\gamma_{c}-3\gamma_{c}^{2}}{4\gamma_{c}^{2}}\geq(1-\gamma_{c})=\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)=\Omega(\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))
, which is
\psi=\mathcal{O}(1)
, we have
{\varphi}_{1}=\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}=\sqrt{e}\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}}{\sqrt{e}+(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, and

\displaystyle{\varphi}_{2}

\displaystyle=1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{\frac{1+\psi}{1+\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{e}}}}=1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}}{1+\frac{(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}})^{2}-1}{\sqrt{e}}}}=1-\frac{0.5(1+\gamma_{c})e^{1/4}}{\sqrt{\sqrt{e}-1+\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}}\right)^{2}}}

\displaystyle=1-\frac{0.5e^{1/4}(2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))}{\sqrt{\sqrt{e}-1+\left(\frac{2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)}{2-2\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)}\right)^{2}}}>1-\gamma_{c}\sqrt{1+\psi}

\displaystyle=1-\gamma_{c}\frac{1+\gamma_{c}}{2\gamma_{c}}=\frac{1-\gamma_{c}}{2}=0.5\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)=\Omega(\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))

{\varphi}_{3}=\frac{L(1+\psi u_{cs}^{2})}{\psi\ell_{cs}^{2}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log(|\mathcal{S}|)(1+\psi)}{\psi}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log(|\mathcal{S}|)}{1-\gamma_{c}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\log(|\mathcal{S}|)}{\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)}\right).

Using
{\varphi}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle w_{t}=\left(1-\frac{\alpha{\varphi}_{2}}{2}\right)^{-t}=\left(1-\alpha/2+\frac{0.25\alpha e^{1/4}(2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma))}{\sqrt{\sqrt{e}-1+\left(\frac{2-\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)}{2-2\mu_{\min}(1-\gamma)}\right)^{2}}}\right)^{-t}.

(141)

Further, we have

l_{cm}=(1+\psi l_{cs}^{2})^{1/2}=\Theta(1)

u_{cm}=(1+\psi u_{cs}^{2})^{1/2}=\Theta(1)

Since TV-divergence is upper bounded by
1
, we have
\bar{m}=\mathcal{O}(1)
. By Lemma
D.3
, we have

A_{1}=A_{2}=2=\mathcal{O}(1)

and
A_{1}=A_{2}=\Omega(1)
,

B=\frac{2}{1-\gamma}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\right).

Hence
m_{1}=2A_{2}\bar{m}=\mathcal{O}(1)
. Also, we have
m_{2}=0
.

We choose the
D
-norm in Lemma
B.9
as the
2
-norm
\|\cdot\|_{2}
. Hence, by primary norm equivalence, we have
l_{cD}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|}}
,
u_{cD}=1
, and hence
\frac{u_{cD}}{l_{cD}}=\sqrt{|\mathcal{S}|}
. The rest of the proof is similar to the proof of Theorem
6.1
where
\mathfrak{I}_{\max}
is substituted with
1
. The sample complexity can also be derived using Corollary
B.1.1
.
∎
